- **.NET Architect**: ASP.NET Core, Blazor, EF Core — Architektur-Entscheidungen, Refactoring-Pläne, Tech-Stack-Abwägungen. Gibt strukturierte Empfehlungen ohne Code zu schreiben. USE FOR: Architektur-Entscheidungen, Solution Design, .NET-Technologieauswahl. DO NOT USE FOR: Code schreiben (use dotnet-developer), Tests planen (use tunit-tester). ([GrexyLoco/K.Agents](https://github.com/GrexyLoco/K.Agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/kagents/agents/dotnet-architect.agent.md))
- **.NET Developer**: C# 14 / .NET 10 development — Blazor components, MAUI views, ASP.NET Core APIs, services, DTOs, dependency injection, middleware. USE FOR: writing, refactoring, and debugging .NET code across Blazor, MAUI, and ASP.NET Core. DO NOT USE FOR: database schema design (use database-engineer), architecture decisions (use app-architect), or writing tests (use tunit-tester). ([GrexyLoco/K.Agents](https://github.com/GrexyLoco/K.Agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/kagents/agents/dotnet-developer.agent.md))
- **.NET Expert**: ASP.NET Core implementation authority. - **3forge-architect**: AMI deployment architect. Plans and scaffolds full 3forge deployments — folder structure, environment configs, property files, and orchestrates sub-agents for schema (3forge-sql-builder), layouts (3forge-layout-architect), and review (3forge-reviewer). Use when the user wants to create a new 3forge project, add environments, or generate a complete deployment scaffold. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-architect.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-config-writer**: AMI configuration file writer. Generates or updates `.properties` files for any AMI component (Center, Web, Relay, WebBalancer, WebManager). Only writes overrides that the project actually requires — AMI runs out of the box with no changes. Use when a project needs custom ports, paths, auth, SSL, persistence directories, or plugin configuration. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-config-writer.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-datasource-advisor**: AMI datasource and feedhandler advisor. Identifies the right integration pattern for connecting external data to AMI, generates CALL __ADD_DATASOURCE stubs for query-based connections, generates Relay feedhandler configuration for streaming/push feeds, and produces custom Java AmiFH/AmiFHBase class skeletons for proprietary data sources. Use when the user wants to connect a new data source to AMI, configure a feedhandler, or implement a custom Java push adapter. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-datasource-advisor.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-layout-architect**: AMI layout architect and builder. Designs and generates .ami layout files — divider tree, datamodel architecture, panel logic, callbacks, and AMIScript. Does not generate styles. Use for all .ami layout generation tasks. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-layout-architect.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-layout-style**: AMI layout visual stylist. Applies or improves the aesthetic design of .ami layout files — color themes, amiStyle properties, styleSets, custom CSS, column color formulas, and HTML panel design. Use when the user wants to style, theme, or visually polish a layout. Can also be called by 3forge-layout-architect after the structural layout is complete. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-layout-style.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-reviewer**: AMI Script code reviewer. Use PROACTIVELY after writing or generating AMI code. Reads files and reviews against all AMI rules — syntax, context (Center vs Web), security, and style. For .ami layout files, validates JSON structure, layout schema (v5/v6), and embedded AMIScript. Reports findings by severity. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/opencode/.opencode/agents/3forge-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-runtime**: Real-time natural language interface to a live AMI deployment. Connects directly to the AMI MCP plugin (Java, port 33333) — no sidecar. Lets users query data, run SQL, inspect tables, manage components, deploy layouts, and interact with Center/Relay/Web via plain English. Delegates schema design to 3forge-sql-builder and layout generation to 3forge-layout-architect. Use when the user wants to talk to a running AMI instance. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-runtime.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-sql-builder**: AMIDB schema builder. Writes config_schema.amisql and managed_schema.amisql files — tables, indexes, triggers, timers, stored procedures, and datasource management. Use when a schema file needs to be created or modified for a 3forge AMI deployment. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-sql-builder.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **4-report-assembler**: Collects all findings from source and compiler analysis, applies supersessions and confidence gates, normalizes IDs, and produces a comprehensive markdown report with structured JSON for downstream tools. - **&lt;repo&gt;-architect**: Use proactively for feature planning and cross-area impact analysis in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Invoke before implementation when a change touches more than one skill's applicability scope, changes shared contracts (proto / schema / public API), or adds new code-gen surface. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-architect.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-maintainer**: Use when a plan or concrete change set is already defined and needs to land in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Follows skill conventions, runs static analysis after each logical change set, and blocks on errors. Standard part of the orchestrated code↔review loop (`/&lt;repo&gt;-implement`). ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-precommit-auditor**: Invoke via `/&lt;repo&gt;-audit` immediately before `git commit` in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Read-only - audits staged changes for commit-readiness (debug prints, stale TODOs introduced in this change, dead comments, leftover scaffolding, AI-cruft comments) and convention drift visible only at the diff level. Complements `&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer` by focusing on commit-ready cleanliness, not code correctness. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-precommit-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer**: Use after the maintainer completes a change set in the &lt;project name&gt; repo - reviews the diff from scratch and produces a classified BLOCKER / WARN / NIT list (correctness, regressions, missing tests, contract drift, convention violations). Read-only - fixes are applied by the maintainer or main context. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **_probe_architect**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent dispatched with `CEO_ARCHITECT_ACTIVE=1` env var is blocked from acting as a parallel CEO architect. Architect role must remain a CEO-only function — never delegated to a subagent. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: architect_role_not_delegable: ..."}`. If the hook allows architect dispatch via native rail, role separation is broken. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_architect.md) · ★2)
- **_probe_canonical_edit**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent attempting to Edit/Write a canonical-guarded path WITHOUT a sentinel approved.md is blocked by `check_canonical_edit.py`. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"CANONICAL-EDIT-BLOCKED: ..."}`. If the canonical hook allows the edit when invoked from the native rail, defense-in-depth is broken and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_canonical_edit.md) · ★2)
- **_probe_missing_skill**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that `check_agent_spawn.py` BLOCKS dispatch via the native subagent rail when the spawn prompt has no skill content (neither `## SKILL CONTENT` inline NOR `## SKILL REFERENCE`). Expected hook output for any Task tool call dispatching this agent: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: missing_skill_content: ..."}`. If the hook ALLOWS this dispatch, native rail has a governance gap and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_missing_skill.md) · ★2)

- **功能代码审查器**: 当需要审查新提交的功能实现代码时使用此agent。该agent将根据项目编码标准分析代码，检查正确性，识别潜在bug，提供改进建议，并给出综合评分（满分10分）。示例： ([halfshade-labs/pytradekit](https://github.com/halfshade-labs/pytradekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **单元测试编写器**: 当新功能或函数已提交到代码库且需要全面单元测试时使用此agent,如果是一些小的改动，比如修改变量名添添加删除注释时，则不需要调用这个agent进行测试。该agent将分析新增代码，在tests/test_{对应文件}.py中使用pytest创建适当的测试文件，编写涵盖边界情况和正常用例的测试，并确保所有测试通过。示例： ([halfshade-labs/pytradekit](https://github.com/halfshade-labs/pytradekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/unit-test-writer.md) · ★1)
- **增量测试编写器**: 当需要为某个Python文件编写测试或分析其测试覆盖情况时使用此agent。该agent会检查tests/test_{对应文件}.py是否存在，分析现有测试，只为缺失的函数补充测试用例。适用于：1) 首次为某个文件编写测试 2) 为已有文件补充缺失的测试覆盖 3) 分析现有测试覆盖情况。不会修改或删除现有测试，只追加缺失部分。注意：如果是为代码中新增的功能编写测试，请改用unit-test-writer。 ([halfshade-labs/pytradekit](https://github.com/halfshade-labs/pytradekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/incremental-test-writer.md) · ★1)
- **安全卫士**: 安全漏洞检测专家。在涉及用户输入、认证、API接口、敏感数据处理的代码变更后自动调用。 ([nongjun/awesome-harness-engineering](https://github.com/nongjun/awesome-harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/%E5%AE%89%E5%85%A8%E5%8D%AB%E5%A3%AB.md) · ★2)
